## 110054: 如果未繪制您的部分或全部資料，則資料會落在可被清除的“可見性范圍”限制之外。 如果您的部分或全部資料屬于地下，則可能需要關閉“高程表面”。 · 描述

**id**　0d7c50f6dca24787e7-1

**english**　110054: If some or all of your data is not drawing, it may fall outside of the Visibility Range limits, which can be cleared. If some or all of your data is underground, you may need to turn off Elevation Surfaces.

## 死胡同掩膜 (制圖工具) · 用法

**id**　0d99a7237454dbbe97-2

**english**　Cul-De-Sac Masks

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 制圖工具箱
- 掩膜工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/cartography/cul-de-sac-masks.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/cartography/cul-de-sac-masks.html

**text**　此工具僅在線的未連接端（亦稱為死巷）創建掩膜。 如果線終點與其他線的端點有共同的端點，則被視為連接。 此工具僅接受線圖層輸入。
在使用圓形端頭來符號化線以平滑線連接的過渡時，此工具用于提供線端掩膜。 如果線端不使用圓形端頭，則可使用此工具創建的面圖徵類來掩膜線端。
通常，邊距大于 0。 如果邊距大小為 0，將創建表示符號化圖徵的確切形狀的掩膜。
如果輸入線圖層包含多部分線幾何，則為所有的未連接線端（包括多部分線內各個部分的線端）創建死胡同掩膜。
創建掩膜時，了解下面這一點非常重要：向地圖添加掩膜將增加地圖的復雜性，從而降低地圖的繪制速度并影響最終的打印和導出。 通常，為地圖創建掩膜時要考慮三個方面：掩膜的數量、掩膜的復雜性，以及是否用于對用標記或線符號填充的面圖徵執行掩膜操作。 掩膜數量的增加、掩膜復雜性的提高和對標記或面填充符號執行掩膜操作都將導致屏幕上的繪制速度降低。 此外，打印和導出兩種操作的性能也會變得很糟，甚至無法生成有效的輸出。 這是因為打印并導出含有掩膜的地圖需要執行大量的處理操作；而且如何使用圖形文件格式存儲具有許多復雜掩膜的地圖導出結果受到已知的限制。
邊距值可使用頁面單位或地圖單位指定。 通常，您需要使用頁面單位來指定邊距值。 所選的單位不同，對邊距值單位的解釋也不同。 如果選擇磅、英寸、毫米或厘米，則掩膜將通過在頁面空間中計算出的邊距來創建（可將邊距視為紙上測量出的距離）。 此計算會將參考比例的參數值納入考量范圍。 如果邊距選用了其他單位，則掩膜將通過在地圖空間中計算出的邊距來創建（可將邊距視為地球上測量出的真實距離）。 并且在這種情況下，計算中不會考慮參考比例參數值。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/cartography/cul-de-sac-masks.html#usage

## 死胡同掩膜 (制圖工具) · 代碼示例

**id**　0d99a7237454dbbe97-20

**english**　Cul-De-Sac Masks

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 制圖工具箱
- 掩膜工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/cartography/cul-de-sac-masks.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/cartography/cul-de-sac-masks.html

**text**　# Name: CulDeSacMasks_standalone_script.py
# Description: Creates masks at the unconnected ends of lines in the input layer.

# Import system modules
import arcpy

# Set environment settings
arcpy.env.workspace = "C:/data"

# Set local variables
input_layer = "cartography.gdb/transportation/roads"
outpuf_fc = "cartography.gdb/transportation/cds_polys"
reference_scale = "25000"
spatial_reference = arcpy.Describe(input_layer).spatialReference
margin = "5 meters"
attributes = "ALL"

# Run Cul De Sac Masks
arcpy.cartography.CulDeSacMasks(input_layer, output_fc, reference_scale,
                                spatial_reference, margin, attributes)

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/cartography/cul-de-sac-masks.html#parameters

## 死胡同掩膜 (制圖工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 制圖工具箱
- 掩膜工具集

**english**　Cul-De-Sac Masks

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/cartography/cul-de-sac-masks.html

**id**　0d99a7237454dbbe97-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/cartography/cul-de-sac-masks.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Cul-De-Sac Masks，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入圖層 [input_layer]、輸出圖徵類 [output_fc]、參考比例 [reference_scale]、計算座標系 [spatial_reference]、邊距 [margin]、傳遞屬性 （可選） [attributes]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/cartography/cul-de-sac-masks.html#parameters

## 死胡同掩膜 (制圖工具) · 英文版工具簽章與參數順序

**english**　Cul-De-Sac Masks

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 制圖工具箱
- 掩膜工具集

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/cartography/cul-de-sac-masks.html

**id**　0d99a7237454dbbe97-schema

**kind**　版本參數表

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/cartography/cul-de-sac-masks.html#parameters

**source_language**　en

**content_version**　3.8

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/cartography/cul-de-sac-masks.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "input_layer", "label_zh": "輸入圖層", "label_en": "Input Layer", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Layer", "description_en": "The input line layer from which the masks will be created."}, {"parameter": "output_fc", "label_zh": "輸出圖徵類", "label_en": "Output Feature Class", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Class", "description_en": "The feature class that will contain the mask features."}, {"parameter": "reference_scale", "label_zh": "參考比例", "label_en": "Reference Scale", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The reference scale used for calculating the masking geometry when masks are specified in page units. This is typically the reference scale of the map."}, {"parameter": "spatial_reference", "label_zh": "計算座標系", "label_en": "Calculation coordinate system", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Spatial Reference", "description_en": "The spatial reference of the map in which the masking polygons will be created. This is not the spatial reference that will be assigned to the output feature class. It is the spatial reference of the map in which the masking polygons will be used, since the position of symbology may change when features are projected."}, {"parameter": "margin", "label_zh": "邊距", "label_en": "Margin", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Linear Unit", "description_en": "The space in page units surrounding the symbolized input features used to create the mask polygons. Typically, masking polygons are created with a small margin around the symbol to improve visual appearance. Margin values are specified in either page units or map units. Most of the time, you will specify your margin distance value in page units. The margin cannot be negative."}, {"parameter": "attributes", "label_zh": "傳遞屬性 （可選）", "label_en": "Transfer Attributes", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the attributes that will be transferred from the input features to the output features. Only feature IDs—Only the FID field from the input features will be transferred to the output features. This is the default. All attributes except feature IDs—All the attributes except the FID from the input features will be transferred to the output features. All attributes—All the attributes from the input features will be transferred to the output features."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.cartography.CulDeSacMasks(input_layer, output_fc, reference_scale, spatial_reference, margin, attributes)

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：input_layer、output_fc、reference_scale、spatial_reference、margin、attributes。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

**text**　雖然此錯誤可能會發生，但發生的頻率非常低，以至于我們尚且無法確定典型原因，也因此目前沒有可用的解決方案。
如果您能夠使用反饋表單來提供有關導致此錯誤的所用步驟的詳細信息，那么此舉將有助于我們在未來確定和提供解決方案。
幫助信息包括下列內容：
消息 ID 以及錯誤或警告消息
工具的名稱
用于參數的值
相關資料集詳細信息
如果該工具不再處于打開狀態，則您可以在地理處理歷史記錄中找到這些詳細信息。
我們對您所提供的一切信息深表感激。 感謝您為我們改進錯誤代碼文檔所做出的貢獻。

**content_version**　3.7

## 001021: 圖徵重疊取決于圖徵真正的幾何質心。 · 描述

**id**　0da4d504c30870ed8e-1

**english**　001021: Feature coincidence is based on feature true geometric centroids.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 1001-1100

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-01001-01025-001021.html#description

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-01001-01025-001021.html

**text**　對于線圖徵和面圖徵，會在計算中使用圖徵真正的幾何質心。對于多點、折線或由多部分組成的面，使用所有圖徵的加權平均中心來計算質心。點圖徵的加權項是 1；線圖徵的加權項是長度；而面圖徵的加權項是面積。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-01001-01025-001021.html#description

## 001021: 圖徵重疊取決于圖徵真正的幾何質心。 · 解決方案

**id**　0da4d504c30870ed8e-2

**english**　001021: Feature coincidence is based on feature true geometric centroids.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 1001-1100

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-01001-01025-001021.html#solution

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-01001-01025-001021.html

**text**　在大多數情況下，不需要求解。然而，如果輸入圖徵類包含多點圖徵并且您想要單獨訪問各個點位置（每個部分），則請先運行 多部分 (Multipart) 至單部分 (Singlepart) 工具，然后運行 收集事件 工具。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-01001-01025-001021.html#solution

## 出口特征服務(資料管理工具) · 摘要摘要

**id**　0daf2ff86771548dcb-1

**english**　Export Feature Service

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 服務工具et

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/export-feature-service-data.html#summary

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/export-feature-service-data.html

**text**　從功能服務向特性服務支持的輸出格式輸出一組資料。 使用此工具導出資料可以保存資料集行為, 如關系、 編輯跟蹤 和其他高級行為, 如 公用網絡或包裹布 。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "Exports a subset of data from a feature service to output formats supported by the feature service.\nExporting data with this tool allows the preservation of dataset behavior such as relationships, editor tracking, and other advanced behaviors such as utility network or parcel fabric.", "section": "Summary"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/data-management/export-feature-service-data.html#summary

## 出口特征服務(資料管理工具) · 使用率

**id**　0daf2ff86771548dcb-2

**english**　Export Feature Service

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 服務工具et

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/export-feature-service-data.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/export-feature-service-data.html

**text**　此工具可以指定具體的表格和特性層。 要使用該工具, 必須在功能服務( Extract) 或網絡特征層( export) 上啟用導出或提取功能。 該工具只支持導出到輸入功能服務所支持的格式。 所有資料都必須來自同一特性服務。 從多個特性服務導出工具, 需要為每個特性服務運行一次。 可用的資料選項由所提供的輸入層和服務能力決定。 選定的控制器資料集所需的額外資料集可能自動包含在輸出中 。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "Specific tables and feature layers can be specified with this tool.\nTo use the tool, export or extract functionality must be enabled on the feature service (extract) or web feature layer (export).\nThis tool only supports exporting to formats that are supported by the input feature service.\nAll data must be from the same feature service. To export from multiple feature services run the tool once for each feature service.\nThe available Data Options parameter options are determined by the provided input layers and the capabilities of the service. Additional datasets required by a selected controller dataset may be automatically included in the output.", "section": "Usage"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/data-management/export-feature-service-data.html#usage

**english**　Export Feature Service

**interface**　dialog

- 標簽標簽
- 解釋解釋解釋解釋
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 幾何過濾器( 可選)
- 用于定義要導出區域的特性層。 如果對幾何過濾層應用了選擇, 則將使用該選擇來確定輸出范圍。 如果沒有在幾何篩選層上選擇, 將使用幾何過濾層的特征范圍。 如果不提供幾何過濾器, 將會導出功能服務資料集的全部范圍 。

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/export-feature-service-data.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/export-feature-service-data.html

**interface**　dialog

- 標簽標簽
- 解釋解釋解釋解釋
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 資料選項( 可選)
- 指定將包含在輸出中的其他資料類型。此參數對應 REST API 中的同步資料選項。可用選項由輸入特征層和表格決定,僅在相應資料出現時才顯示。包括工具網絡 - 輸出資料將包括實用網絡。包含 Parcel Fabrics - 輸出資料將包括包件織物。

## 出口特征服務(資料管理工具) · 參數

**id**　0daf2ff86771548dcb-13

**english**　Export Feature Service

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 服務工具et

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/export-feature-service-data.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/export-feature-service-data.html

**parameter**　related_data_options

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標簽標簽
- 解釋解釋解釋解釋
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 相關資料選項(備選)
- 指定在輸入資料集列表中包含的目標資料集在參與關系類時如何處理。例如,當過濾器內有功能(f1),而過濾器外有另一個類別的相關特性(f2)時,如果選擇獲取相關資料,則該功能F2將包括在導出中。如果源資料集導出時沒有過濾器,此選項可能導致包含大量相關的行。默認 - 使用特性層選項或表格選項參數中規定的行為。如果對目標資料集應用過濾器,則使用這些過濾器。如果沒有應用過濾器,則所有記錄都將包含在內。如果需要的話,還包含與包括源行的有關目標行。以維護關系完整性。如果指定 SCHEMA_ONLY 選項,則只導出 schema。這是默認的。相關的行只包含輸入資料集列表中與特征類別和表格有關的記錄。對目的地資料集適用的幾何過濾器和定義查詢將被忽略。
- 字符串

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"values": ["Related Data Options (Optional)", "Specifies how destination datasets that are included in the input dataset list will be processed when they participate in relationship classes. For example, when a feature (f1) is within the filter and a related feature (f2) from another class is outside the filter, the feature f2 will be included in the export if you choose to get related data. If the origin dataset is exported without filters, this option can result in a large number of related rows being included. Default—Uses the behavior specified by the Feature Layer Options or Table Options parameters. If filters are applied to the destination dataset, those filters are used. If no filters are applied, all records are included. Related destination rows associated with included origin rows are also included when required to preserve relationship integrity. If the SCHEMA_ONLY option is specified, only the schema is exported. This is the default. Related rows only—Records related to the feature classes and tables in the input dataset list will be included. Geometry filters and definition queries applied to destination datasets will be ignored.", "String"], "section": "Parameters"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/data-management/export-feature-service-data.html#parameters

## 出口特征服務(資料管理工具) · 參數

**id**　0daf2ff86771548dcb-14

**english**　Export Feature Service

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 服務工具et

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/export-feature-service-data.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/export-feature-service-data.html

**parameter**　expand_related_data_options

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標簽標簽
- 解釋解釋解釋解釋
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 擴展相關資料選項(備選)
- 指定是否將輸入資料集參數中未指定的目的地資料集添加到輸出中。 不要添加 - 目的地特征分類或表格。 僅導出輸入資料集參數中指定的資料集。 這是默認的。 從輸入資料集參數中指定的目標特性類別或表格中只添加相關的行。 只會導出與輸入資料集參數中指定的資料集有關的相關行。 添加時, 僅使用 schema 。 只有輸入資料集參數中指定的目標特征類別和表格的圖案才會導出。 添加所有行 - 所有目標特征類別或表格中與輸入資料集中指定的資料集相關的所有行。參數將導出。
- 字符串

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"values": ["Expand Related Data Options (Optional)", "Specifies whether the destination datasets not specified in the Input Datasets parameter will be added to the output. Do not add—Destination feature classes or tables will not be exported. Only datasets specified in the Input Datasets parameter will be exported. This is the default. Add related rows only—Related rows from the destination feature classes or tables related to the datasets specified in the Input Datasets parameter will be exported. Add with schema only—Only the schema for the destination feature classes and tables related to the datasets specified in the Input Datasets parameter will be exported. Add all rows—All rows from the destination feature classes or tables related to the datasets specified in the Input Datasets parameter will be exported.", "String"], "section": "Parameters"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/data-management/export-feature-service-data.html#parameters

**english**　Export Feature Service

- 姓名名稱名稱名稱

**values**　
- 輸出類型( 可選)
- 指定工具將創建的輸出類型。 FILE_GDB - 資料將導出到文件地理資料庫。這是默認的。 MOBILE_GDB - 資料將導出到移動地理資料庫。 SHAPEFILE - 資料將導出到形狀文件。 GEOJSON - 資料將導出到 GeoJSON 文件。 CSV - 資料將導出到. csv 文件。 KML - 資料將導出到. kml 文件 。

MOBILE_GDB—The data will be exported to a mobile geodatabase. SHAPEFILE—The data will be exported to a shapefile. GEOJSON—The data will be exported to a GeoJSON file. CSV—The data will be exported to a .csv file. KML—The data will be exported to a .kml file.", "String"], "section": "Derived output"}

**interface**　python

- 姓名名稱名稱名稱
- 解釋解釋解釋解釋

**values**　
- 資料選項 [資料選項,...] (備選)
- 指定輸出中將包含的其他資料類型。該參數與REST API中的同步資料選項相對應。可用選項由輸入特征層和表格確定,僅在相應資料出現時才顯示。 INCLUDE_UTILITRIY_NETWORK - 輸出資料將包括通用網絡。 INCLUDE_PARCEL_FABRICS - 輸出資料將包括包件織物。

This parameter corresponds to the Sync Data Options in the REST API. The available options are determined by the input feature layers and tables and only appear when the corresponding data is present. INCLUDE_UTILITY_NETWORK—The output data will include utility networks. INCLUDE_PARCEL_FABRICS—The output data will include parcel fabrics.", "String"], "section": "Derived output"}

## 出口特征服務(資料管理工具) · 衍生輸出

**id**　0daf2ff86771548dcb-27

**english**　Export Feature Service

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 服務工具et

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/export-feature-service-data.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/export-feature-service-data.html

**parameter**　related_data_options

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 姓名名稱名稱名稱
- 解釋解釋解釋解釋
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 相關_ data_ 選項( 可選)
- 指定輸入資料集列表中包含的目標資料集在參與關系類時將如何處理。例如,當過濾器內有一個功能(f1),而另一個類別的相關特性(f2)不在過濾器之外時,如果選擇獲取相關資料,則該功能F2將包括在導出中。如果源資料集在沒有過濾器的情況下導出,此選項可能導致大量相關的行被包括在內。 DEFAUT - 使用特性_layer_options或表_options參數指定的行為。如果對目標資料集應用過濾器,這些過濾器將被使用。如果不應用過濾器,則包含所有記錄。如果指定了“只有Schema only”選項,則僅導出該預案。這是默認的。輸入資料集列表中與特征類別和表格相關的相關記錄將包括在內。對目的地資料集應用的幾何過濾器和定義查詢將被忽略。
- 字符串

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"values": ["related_data_options (Optional)", "Specifies how destination datasets that are included in the input dataset list will be processed when they participate in relationship classes. For example, when a feature (f1) is within the filter and a related feature (f2) from another class is outside the filter, the feature f2 will be included in the export if you choose to get related data. If the origin dataset is exported without filters, this option can result in a large number of related rows being included. DEFAULT—Uses the behavior specified by the feature_layer_options or table_options parameters. If filters are applied to the destination dataset, those filters are used. If no filters are applied, all records are included. Related destination rows associated with included origin rows are also included when required to preserve relationship integrity. If the Schema Only option is specified, only the schema is exported. This is the default. RELATED_ROWS_ONLY—Records related to the feature classes and tables in the input dataset list will be included. Geometry filters and definition queries applied to destination datasets will be ignored.", "String"], "section": "Derived output"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/data-management/export-feature-service-data.html#derived-output

## 出口特征服務(資料管理工具) · 衍生輸出

**id**　0daf2ff86771548dcb-28

**english**　Export Feature Service

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 服務工具et

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/export-feature-service-data.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/export-feature-service-data.html

**parameter**　expand_related_data_options

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 姓名名稱名稱名稱
- 解釋解釋解釋解釋
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 擴展的 _相關_data_選項(可選擇)
- 指定是否在 _ dataset 參數中未指定的目的地資料集將添加到輸出中 。 DO_ NOT_ADD- Destendation 特征類或表格將不會導出。 只有 in_ datas 參數中指定的資料集才會導出。 這是默認的。 來自 目的 特性類或表格中指定的資料類或表格中指定的資料類將導出。 ADD_ WITH_SCHEMA_ONLY - 僅指目標特性類和與in_ datas 參數中指定的資料組相關的表格。 ADD_ALL_ROWS - 將導出目的地特征類別或表格中與輸入資料集參數中指定的資料集相關的所有行。
- 字符串

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"values": ["expand_related_data_options (Optional)", "Specifies whether the destination datasets not specified in the in_datasets parameter will be added to the output. DO_NOT_ADD—Destination feature classes or tables will not be exported. Only datasets specified in the in_datasets parameter will be exported. This is the default. ADD_RELATED_ROWS_ONLY—Related rows from the destination feature classes or tables related to the datasets specified in the in_datasets parameter will be exported. ADD_WITH_SCHEMA_ONLY—Only the schema for the destination feature classes and tables related to the datasets specified in the in_datasets parameter will be exported. ADD_ALL_ROWS—All rows from the destination feature classes or tables related to the datasets specified in the in_datasets parameter will be exported.", "String"], "section": "Derived output"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/data-management/export-feature-service-data.html#derived-output

    output_type="FILE_GDB",
    geometry_filter=None,
    feature_layer_options="USE_FILTERS",
    table_options="USE_FILTERS",
    include_attachments="INCLUDE_ATTACHMENTS",
    data_options=None,
    related_data_options="DEFAULT",
    expand_related_data_options="DO_NOT_ADD"
)

**kind**　版本參數表

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/export-feature-service-data.html#parameters

**source_language**　en

**content_version**　3.8

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/data-management/export-feature-service-data.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "in_datasets", "label_zh": "輸入資料集", "label_en": "Input Datasets", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Table View; Feature Layer", "description_en": "The input table views or feature layers that will be exported."}, {"parameter": "out_location", "label_zh": "產出 產出", "label_en": "Output Location", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Folder", "description_en": "The location of the database or the folder that will be created for the exported datasets."}, {"parameter": "output_name", "label_zh": "輸出名稱", "label_en": "Output Name", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The name of the database or the folder that will be created for the exported datasets."}, {"parameter": "output_type", "label_zh": "輸出類型( 可選)", "label_en": "Output Type (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the output type that will be created by the tool. File geodatabase—The data will be exported to a file geodatabase. This is the default. Mobile geodatabase—The data will be exported to a mobile geodatabase. Shapefile—The data will be exported to a shapefile. GeoJSON—The data will be exported to a GeoJSON file. CSV—The data will be exported to a .csv file. KML—The data will be exported to a .kml file."}, {"parameter": "geometry_filter", "label_zh": "幾何過濾器( 可選)", "label_en": "Geometry Filter (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Feature Layer", "description_en": "The feature layer that will define the area to export. If a selection is applied to the geometry filter layer, the selection will be used to define the export extent. If there is no selection on the geometry filter layer, the feature extent of the geometry filter layer will be used. If no geometry filter is provided, the full extent of the feature service dataset will be exported."}, {"parameter": "feature_layer_options", "label_zh": "功能圖層選項( 可選)", "label_en": "Feature Layer Options (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies how input feature layers will be exported. Use filters—Uses the geometry filter in the tool along with the definition query applied to the layer in the input list. This is the default. All records—All records are retrieved for datasets in the input list. Schema only—Only return the schema for datasets in the input list."}, {"parameter": "table_options", "label_zh": "表格選項(備選)", "label_en": "Table Options (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies how input tables will be exported. Use filters—Uses the definition query applied to the layer in the input list. This is the default. All records—All records are retrieved for tables in the input list. Schema only—Only return the schema for tables in the input list."}, {"parameter": "include_attachments", "label_zh": "包含附件( 可選擇的)", "label_en": "Include Attachments (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether attachments will be included in the output. Checked—Attachments will be included. This is the default. Unchecked—Attachments will not be included."}, {"parameter": "data_options", "label_zh": "資料選項( 可選)", "label_en": "Data Options (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the additional types of data that will be included in the output. This parameter corresponds to the Sync Data Options in the REST API. The available options are determined by the input feature layers and tables and only appear when the corresponding data is present. Include Utility Network—The output data will include utility networks. Include Parcel Fabrics—The output data will include parcel fabrics."}, {"parameter": "related_data_options", "label_zh": "相關資料選項(備選)", "label_en": "Related Data Options (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies how destination datasets that are included in the input dataset list will be processed when they participate in relationship classes. For example, when a feature (f1) is within the filter and a related feature (f2) from another class is outside the filter, the feature f2 will be included in the export if you choose to get related data. If the origin dataset is exported without filters, this option can result in a large number of related rows being included. Default—Uses the behavior specified by the Feature Layer Options or Table Options parameters. If filters are applied to the destination dataset, those filters are used. If no filters are applied, all records are included. Related destination rows associated with included origin rows are also included when required to preserve relationship integrity. If the SCHEMA_ONLY option is specified, only the schema is exported. This is the default. Related rows only—Records related to the feature classes and tables in the input dataset list will be included. Geometry filters and definition queries applied to destination datasets will be ignored."}, {"parameter": "expand_related_data_options", "label_zh": "擴展相關資料選項(備選)", "label_en": "Expand Related Data Options (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies whether the destination datasets not specified in the Input Datasets parameter will be added to the output. Do not add—Destination feature classes or tables will not be exported. Only datasets specified in the Input Datasets parameter will be exported. This is the default. Add related rows only—Related rows from the destination feature classes or tables related to the datasets specified in the Input Datasets parameter will be exported. Add with schema only—Only the schema for the destination feature classes and tables related to the datasets specified in the Input Datasets parameter will be exported. Add all rows—All rows from the destination feature classes or tables related to the datasets specified in the Input Datasets parameter will be exported."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.management.ExportFeatureService(in_datasets, out_location, output_name, {output_type}, {geometry_filter}, {feature_layer_options}, {table_options}, {include_attachments}, {data_options}, {related_data_options}, {expand_related_data_options})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：in_datasets、out_location、output_name、output_type、geometry_filter、feature_layer_options、table_options、include_attachments、data_options、related_data_options、expand_related_data_options。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

## 出口特征服務(資料管理工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 資料處理工具
- 資料管理工具箱
- 服務工具et

**english**　Export Feature Service

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/export-feature-service-data.html

**id**　0daf2ff86771548dcb-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/data-management/export-feature-service-data.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Export Feature Service，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入資料集 [in_datasets]、產出 產出 [out_location]、輸出名稱 [output_name]、輸出類型( 可選) [output_type]、幾何過濾器( 可選) [geometry_filter]、功能圖層選項( 可選) [feature_layer_options]、表格選項(備選) [table_options]、包含附件( 可選擇的) [include_attachments]、資料選項( 可選) [data_options]、相關資料選項(備選) [related_data_options]、擴展相關資料選項(備選) [expand_related_data_options]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/data-management/export-feature-service-data.html#parameters

## 045019：參數“至少包含”超過了輸入資料集中可用點的數量。 該值已減少至輸入資料集中包含的點數。 · 描述

**id**　0db1153d0a6a3d70b4-1

**english**　045019: Parameter "Include at least" exceeds the number of points available from the input dataset. It was decreased to the number of points in the input dataset.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 45001-45100

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/040001-050000/tool-errors-and-warnings-45001-45025-045019.html#description

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/040001-050000/tool-errors-and-warnings-45001-45025-045019.html

**text**　如果“至少包含”的值超過輸入資料集中資料的數量，則會顯示該警告。 在執行計算之前，要包含的資料量會自動縮減以匹配輸入資料集中資料的數量。

**content_version**　3.7

需具備 Spatial Analyst 許可方可使用。
地圖代數是通過使用代數語言創建表達式以執行空間分析的一種方法。 使用網格計算器工具，您可以創建和運行能夠輸出網格資料集的地圖代數表達式。
